## Luxury Soda Commercial Video Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a luxury beverage commercial featuring a single {argument name="soda color" default="purple"} soda can standing perfectly upright at the center of the frame on a glossy reflective surface. The environment is dark violet with soft bokeh particles and dramatic studio lighting.
Scene 1 – Stillness (0–2s):
The video opens in complete stillness. Only the can is visible—no berries, no water, no splashes, no particles. Condensation beads glisten on the surface. Subtle rim lighting outlines the can while the background remains moody and minimal.
Scene 2 – Tension Build (2–4s):
A low-frequency vibration begins. The can starts to shake gently, then slightly more intensely, as if pressure is building inside. The camera remains locked and centered, heightening anticipation.
Scene 3 – Burst Moment (4–7s):
The top of the can suddenly bursts open in slow motion. A powerful surge of luminous purple liquid erupts upward. The motion is controlled and cinematic—no chaotic spray. The liquid forms elegant arcs and sculpted waves.
Scene 4 – Flavor Reveal (7–10s):
As the purple liquid rises, glossy grape berries emerge from the opening, carried by the splash. Droplets and fruit are suspended in ultra-slow motion. The splash wraps around the can without hiding it, maintaining full product visibility.
Scene 5 – Hero Composition (10–14s):
The liquid reaches its peak and frames the can in a dramatic crown shape. Berries float in perfect balance around the splash. The reflective surface below mirrors the entire scene, creating depth and luxury.
Final Hold (14–16s):
Motion settles into a striking hero shot: can centered, purple liquid frozen in sculptural form, berries suspended mid-air. No text, no logos, no transitions—pure visual impact.
Style & Mood:
Ultra-premium soda advertisement, dark luxury aesthetic, cinematic slow motion, high-contrast lighting, glossy reflections, dramatic yet elegant energy.
Camera & Technical Direction:
Locked-off camera, high-speed macro cinematography, realistic fluid dynamics, controlled splash physics, volumetric lighting, shallow depth of field, hyper-detailed droplets.
```

## Editorial Portrait Contact Sheet Aesthetic

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photorealistic editorial-style portrait composed as a four-frame grid, resembling a contact sheet from a film photoshoot. Each frame features the same young woman posing against a neutral gray studio wall, captured in slightly different natural poses and expressions.
She has voluminous dark hair styled loose with soft movement, strong brows, defined cheekbones, and natural glam makeup emphasizing her eyes and lips. She is wearing a fitted white tank top with a deep neckline, cropped just above the waist, revealing subtle midriff in some frames.
Across the frames, her poses vary: one with a hand lightly gripping the fabric near her chest, another standing straight with relaxed shoulders, another with arms raised above her head, and one adjusting the hem of the top. Her expressions range from calm and confident to softly introspective.
The lighting is soft but direct, similar to on-camera flash, creating gentle highlights on skin and fabric while preserving natural texture. The image includes visible film grain, dust, and minor imperfections, enhancing a vintage analog photography aesthetic. The overall mood is confident, sensual in a restrained editorial way, and timeless.

Style & Quality Cues
Photorealistic, fashion editorial photography, film contact sheet aesthetic, subtle grain, natural skin texture, high detail, minimal retouching

Lighting
Soft direct flash, even illumination, low ambient shadows, realistic contrast

Camera
Medium portrait framing, eye-level angle, shallow depth of field, 35mm film look

Negative Prompt
 exaggerated anatomy, glossy commercial lighting, heavy retouching, plastic skin, cartoon or illustration style, AI artifacts, distortion
```

## High-End Editorial Beauty Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-end editorial beauty portrait in a controlled studio setting, focused on emotional intimacy and tactile realism. Tight close-up framing from head to clavicle, vertical orientation, with the subject as the sole visual anchor.
85mm telephoto equivalent, eye-level camera position, close working distance. Chosen to compress facial features, soften contours, and create a cinematic, luxurious portrait. Shallow depth of field at f/1.8 for strong background separation and creamy bokeh.
Soft directional key light from camera-left, slightly above eye level, sculpting facial planes and hair volume. Minimal fill light to preserve shadow depth without losing detail. Subtle rim/back light for clean subject–background separation. Medium-low contrast, controlled shadows. Cinematic, low-key editorial mood.
Warm, natural skin tones contrasted against a cool dark-gray background. Subtle cinematic color grading with smooth tonal transitions; no aggressive stylization.
Long, wavy hair with intentional imperfection, fine side braids and {argument name="ribbon color" default="black"} satin ribbons as secondary details. Semi-transparent {argument name="top color" default="black"} knit top with visible weave texture. Hand gently touching face/chin, relaxed elegant pose with slight lean toward camera.
Low ISO for maximum tonal cleanliness. Editorial sharpness on hair and fabric, natural skin rendering with light professional retouching. Photorealistic, cinematic, luxury fashion editorial standard.
```

## Day vs. Night Split Portrait Comparison

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Before-and-after upper-body portrait photo of Sydney Sweeney, smiling in both images, split vertically down the center into two equal halves for a direct visual comparison. Clear text labels appear at the top of each half.

Left side – “Day” (label at top):
Natural, no makeup look. Realistic skin texture with visible pores, slight under-eye shadows, natural lips, minimal grooming. Soft, relaxed smile. Hair casually styled. She is wearing a simple nightgown for bed, comfortable and modest. Daytime feeling, fresh and unstyled, authentic at-home appearance.

Right side – “Night” (label at top):
Professional makeup applied: even skin tone, subtle contouring, enhanced eyes with mascara and eyeliner, defined brows, soft lipstick. Bright, confident smile. Hair styled elegantly. She is wearing a beautiful, pink dress with a deep neckline

Both sides show the same pose, framing, camera distance, background, and angle to ensure the contrast comes purely from makeup, styling, and wardrobe.

Photography & Camera Style:
Smartphone camera realism, portrait mode look, handheld feel, natural lighting, slight digital noise, mild compression compression artifacts, realistic color balance, minimal retouching, honest skin detail. Casual but clear comparison photo rather than studio perfection.

Aspect ratio:
3:4 (portrait)

negative prompt:
beauty filters, over-smoothed skin, exaggerated makeup, inconsistent lighting, mismatched expressions, face distortion, studio glamour lighting, cinematic look, watermark, text artifacts (except “Day” and “Night” labels)
```

## New Chinese Wuxia Style Fashion Shoot Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
As a world-class photographer (Ren Hang / Chen Man / Tim Walker / Jimmy Nelson / Steve McCurry), create a new Chinese Wuxia style fashion blockbuster set in a landscape for **{argument name="character description" default="the character in Reference Image 1"}**, showcasing the “New Wuxia Spirit in the Mountains and Rivers.”

Core Principles
Reference Image 1 (100% Retention):

All appearance features: face shape, facial features, age, body type, skin tone
Hairstyle features: strictly follow the actual hairstyle in Reference Image 1

Reference Image 2 (Precise Extraction):

Core style: New Chinese Wuxia fashion blockbuster
Hairstyle: Tied-up style
Clothing: Light-colored loose long gown + polka dot pants + thick-soled boots
Core prop: Gourd tied with a red silk ribbon
Core pose: Standing sideways with hands behind back, leaning against a waterfall
Environment: Giant multi-tiered waterfall + mossy rock wall
Composition: Large scene, small subject
Color tone: Cool cyan-green + light off-white + red accents
Atmosphere: Cold, isolated, Wuxia spirit

I. Character Appearance Restoration (Reference Image 1)
Face shape and features
✅ 100% pixel-level restoration of Reference Image 1

Facial contour, facial width, jawline
Eyes, nose, lips, ears - fully retained
Cheekbone position, facial dimensionality
Skin tone, age features, body type

Hairstyle
✅ Reference Image 1 hairstyle + tied-up treatment
Basic hairstyle:

Fully retain the hairstyle from Reference Image 1

Tied-up treatment (Reference Image 2):

Tied-up style

Hair tied back
Secured with a hairband or hairpin
Possibly on top of the head or back of the neck
Iconic Wuxia style
Conveys a “disciplined heroic feel”

Tied-up style:

High bun or half-bun
Neat and sharp
Possibly with a hair knot
Wuxia demeanor

II. Clothing Design - New Chinese Wuxia (Reference Image 2 Core)
Main clothing - Light-colored loose long gown
✅ Flowing and unrestrained
Gown features:

Color: Light beige, off-white, or light gray
Style:

Loose fit
Long length (past knee or mid-calf)
Chinese stand collar or cross collar
Flowing feel

Material:

Lightweight fabric (cotton linen or silk)
Flowing, draped
Wuxia feel

Details:

Possibly frog buttons
Wide sleeves
Flowing hem

Pants - Polka dot pants
✅ Modern fashion integration
Pants features:

Pattern: Polka dots (circles)

Dark base + light dots
Or light base + dark dots

Style: Loose or slim fit
Function: Modern element integrated into Chinese style

Shoes - Thick-soled boots
✅ Ancient and modern combination
Boots features:

Thick sole design
Black or dark color
Balances ancient style with modern fashion

Symbolic meaning:

Long gown = Traditional Wuxia spirit
Polka dot pants = New Chinese fashion integration
Thick-soled boots = Collision of ancient and modern

III. Core Prop - Gourd tied with a red silk ribbon (Reference Image 2 Core)
Gourd
✅ Chinese Wuxia symbol
Gourd features:

Shape: Traditional gourd shape

Small top, large bottom
Tightened waist

Material:

Natural gourd or ceramic
Rustic texture
Possibly with patterns or textures

Color:

Dark brown, yellowish-brown
Natural tones

Red Silk Ribbon
✅ Vibrant accent
Red ribbon features:

Color: {argument name="ribbon color" default="vivid red"}

High saturation
The only bright color in the image

Tying method:

Tied around the neck or waist of the gourd
Flowing, dynamic
Possibly with tassels

Effect:

Adds dynamism
Vibrant accent
Breaks the cool tone

Holding method:

Holding the gourd
Or lifting the red ribbon
Natural, casual

Symbolic meaning:

Gourd = Symbol of a wandering hero, casualness
Red ribbon = Passion, heroism, dynamism

IV. Core Pose - Standing Sideways with Hands Behind Back (Reference Image 2 Core)
Pose
✅ Isolated relaxation, solitary wandering hero
Standing posture:

Standing sideways

Body angled towards the camera
Not frontal
Shows the side silhouette

Hands behind back (optional)

One or both hands behind the back
The other hand might hold the gourd
Relaxed, composed

Position:

Leaning against a waterfall or rock wall

Standing in front of the waterfall
Standing on a rock
Integrated with the landscape

Head direction:

Sideways or slightly turned towards the camera
Eyes looking into the distance
Or looking straight ahead

Overall temperament:

Isolated, relaxed
"Solitary wandering hero" temperament
Cold, unrestrained

V. Environment Design - Giant Waterfall Landscape (Reference Image 2 Standard)
Core Scene
✅ Giant multi-tiered waterfall + mossy rock wall
Waterfall features:

Giant waterfall

Multi-tiered waterfall
Pouring down from a height
Rapid water flow
Grand, spectacular

Mist pervasive

Mist created by the waterfall
Fills the entire scene
Adds an ethereal feel

Rock wall features:

Mossy rocks

Covered in dark green moss
Hemid, rustic
Uneven texture

Layered rocks

Multi-layered structure
Strong sense of depth

Vegetation:

Green moss
Possibly ferns
Enhances the landscape atmosphere

VI. Composition Design - Large Scene, Small Subject (Reference Image 2 Standard)
Core Composition
✅ Use the grand landscape to highlight the character's isolation
Character proportion:

Small proportion subject

Character occupies 10-20% of the frame
Positioned towards the right or left
Not centered

Waterfall proportion:

Giant background

Waterfall occupies 70-80% of the frame
Visual core
Grand, shocking

Composition effect:

Uses the grand landscape to contrast the character
Reinforces the sense of "isolation"
The hero's smallness and boldness within nature

Layout

Character: Bottom right or bottom left
Waterfall: Occupies most of the frame
Top: Sky or brand text
Bottom: Theme text (optional)

VII. Lighting Design - Cool Diffused Light (Reference Image 2 Core)
Main Light - Natural Cool Tone Light
✅ Cool texture of mountains and rivers
Light source setting:

Light source: Natural light (overcast or forest light)
Light quality: Diffused light, soft light
Color temperature: Cool tone (5500-7000K)
Effect: Cold, misty texture

Lighting Effect
✅ Cool landscape atmosphere
Waterfall lighting:

Cool white light
Misty and hazy water
Clear layers

Character lighting:

Soft and even
Light-colored long gown is bright
Clear silhouette

Overall atmosphere:

Cold, isolated
Ethereal feel of the landscape

VIII. Color Scheme - Cool Cyan-Green + Red Accent (Reference Image 2 Standard)
Main Color Tone
✅ Cool cyan-green + light off-white + red
Color distribution:
Background (Dominant):

Cyan-green (#4A7C78 / #5F9EA0)

Waterfall flow
Mossy rock wall
Vegetation
Cool tone, cold

Flowing feel

{argument name="accent color" default="vivid red"} (#DC143C / #E60012)

Red ribbon
The only bright color in the image
High saturation 90-100%
Visual focus

Overall color tone:

"Heroic passion hidden within the cold silence of the landscape"
Cool cyan-green 70%
Light off-white 30%
Red accent <5%

Contrast

60-75%
Cool but layered

Saturation

Cyan-green: Medium (40-60%)
Light off-white: Low (20-30%)
Red ribbon: Extremely high (90-100%)

IX. Texture and Post-Processing
Hairstyle Texture
✅ Neat tied-up hair

Hair tied up neatly
Clear hair strands
Disciplined heroic feel

Clothing Texture
✅ Flowing and light

Gown fabric drapes well
Flowing feel
Possibly fluttering in the wind

Clear gourd texture
Silky sheen of the red ribbon
Flowing dynamics

Misty water haze

Step 1: Color Grading

Enhance cool cyan-green tones

Increase red saturation to 90-100%
Becomes the visual focus

Increase haziness

Sharpen the waterfall layers

"{argument name="brand logo" default="SHANG CHENG SHI"}"

## Soft Glam Executive Portrait with Identity Preservation

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded photo STRICTLY as the facial identity reference. Preserve the same woman exactly — no change to facial structure, age, skin tone, or defining features.\
\
Create a cinematic 9:16 vertical ultra-realistic executive portrait of a confident, modern professional woman standing on a contemporary city terrace beside a sleek reflective glass wall. The background features softly blurred blue-gray skyscrapers, creating a refined corporate-luxury atmosphere.\
\
Lighting is soft glam and flattering: diffused natural daylight combined with subtle fill light, creating smooth highlights, gentle shadows, and luminous yet realistic skin texture. The mood is calm, powerful, and elegant.\
\
Styling & outfit:\
• Tailored {argument name="blouse color" default="deep red or ivory"} silk blouse with a clean neckline\
• High-waisted black tailored trousers with a sharp executive silhouette\
• Minimal black leather belt with polished metal buckle\
• Elegant silver or rose-gold wristwatch\
\
Hair & makeup:\
• Neatly styled hair (soft waves or sleek low ponytail)\
• Soft glam makeup: natural base, subtle contour, neutral eyes, defined brows, nude or soft rose lips\
\
Pose & body language:\
• Upright, confident posture\
• One hand lightly resting in the trouser pocket\
• Other arm relaxed by the side\
• Eye-level medium shot with composed, leadership-focused expression\
\
Camera & quality:\
Full-frame cinematic look, 35–50mm lens, f/2.0, ISO 100–300, 5400K white balance. Ultra-clean cinematic realism, premium editorial finish, shallow depth of field, sharp subject focus, professional executive photography, 4K–8K quality.",
  "negative_prompt": "face change, identity alteration, heavy glamour makeup, exaggerated posing, cartoon, CGI, illustration, plastic skin, over-retouching, distortion, blur, low resolution, watermark, logo, text, harsh lighting"
}
```

{
  "prompt": "Ultra photo-realistic full-body street-style photograph of a natural female model in her late 20s wearing a {argument name="garment color" default="charcoal gray"} asymmetric wool poncho identical to the reference, with elegant draped folds on one shoulder. The model is different from all previous references — she has shoulder-length wavy chestnut-brown hair, warm beige skin tone, and a relaxed, confident look. She is photographed outdoors in soft daylight, standing casually on a European street with a stone or marble wall background, giving a modern yet natural vibe. The outfit includes straight dark jeans, ankle boots, and a small leather crossbody bag. Lighting is soft natural daylight (around 5200 K), evenly diffused to highlight the wool texture and subtle shadows. Skin should look realistic with visible texture, eyes clear, and proportions humanly accurate. Captured with a DSLR 50 mm lens, f/4, ISO 200 for authentic focus depth. The result must look indistinguishable from a real photo — true fabric details, lifelike model expression, balanced exposure, and photographic realism.",
  "negative_prompt": "no stylized AI faces, no over-smooth skin, no symmetry artifacts, no HDR glow, no plastic look, no fantasy background, no motion blur, no duplicated limbs, no flat lighting, no cropped frame, no uncanny valley aesthetics.",

  }

Create a high-fashion studio portrait of a man, using the attached reference photo for face and appearance, with a bold, artistic aesthetic.

The man is captured in sharp side profile, standing against a monochromatic gradient background blending deep violet and smoky gray, evoking a mysterious, futuristic mood.

Charcoal gray turtleneck, elongating the neck and creating dramatic contrast with the background.

Lighting:

Moody, sculptural shadows emphasizing cheekbones and jawline.

Subtle purple-blue rim light outlining the face, adding depth and a futuristic glow.

Art direction:

Inspired by Vogue Homme and Numero Homme editorial photography.

A balance of elegance, avant-garde, and surreal minimalism.

Technical details: Leica SL2 + 85mm

f/1.2 lens, aperture f/2.0, ISO 100, shutter 1/200s.

Ultra-high resolution 8K, perfect for luxury magazine covers.
```
